=== Drive-asymmetry runs: the gain g = ln(response ratio)/ln(drive ratio) between the halves x < L/2 and x >= L/2 ===
Per half h, free-volume bookkeeping is exact: R_h eta <d_emit>_h = P_h (<d_w>_h - <d_old>_h) - dS_h, so
g_R - 1 = [ln(inj0/inj1) - ln(demit0/demit1) + (drift term)] / ln(P0/P1), with inj = <d_w> - <d_old> per primary.
The drift part is the change of each half's total free volume over the finite window; it averages to zero over long
windows and seeds, so g_inf = 1 + injection part + emission part estimates the long-window gain. The injection part
splits into a write part (more aligned writes in the driven half) and an overwrite part (looser sites overwritten).
run | eta | A | P0/P1 | g_E (events incl. primaries) | g_R (repacks) | injection part (write, overwrite) | emission part | drift part | g_inf | 1 - 1/A | dbar0 dbar1 | demit0 demit1
